// Default material\n    world.materials.emplace_back(Material{0.8, 0.5});    // Inflow material\n    world.materials.emplace_back(Material{0.8, -0.5});   // Outflow material\n    \n    // Allocate world.elements_dynamic[i].current_energy = 0.0;\n        world.elements_dynamic[i].total_flux = 0.0;\n    }\n    \n    // Build connectivity: each element connects to its neighbors in 2D grid\n    for     for (int n = 0; n < 4; ++n) {\n                const int nx = x + offsets[n][0];\n                const int ny = y + offsets[n][1];\n                \n                // Check if neighbor is    }\n        }\n    }\n    \n    // Set corner elements as inflow/outflow to create interesting dynamics\n    const int last = n_elems_root - 1;\n    world.elements_static[0 * n_elems_root +    world.elements_static[last * n_elems_root + last].material_idx = INFLOW_MAT_ID;\n}\n\n// Compute energy flux between two elements\ninline val_t computeFlux(const Material& mat, const ElementDynamic& this_elem,\n                        val_t connection_flux, const ElementDynamic& other_elem) {\n    return (other_elem.current_energy - this_elem.current_energy) * \n world.elements_static.size();\n    \n    for (int iter = 0; iter < n_iters; ++iter) {\n        // Update all elements\n        for (size_t i = 0; i < n_elems; ++i) {\n            const ElementStatic& elem_static.num_connections; ++j) {\n                const idx_t neighbor_idx = elem_static.connected_idx[j];\n                const ElementDynamic& neighbor_dyn = world.elements_dynamic[neighbor_idx];\n                total_flux += computeFlux(mat, elem_dyn, elem_static.connected_flux[j], neighbor_dyn);\n            }\n            \n            // Update element state\n            ElementDynamic& elem_write = world.elements_dynamic_swap[i];\n            elem_write.current_energy = elem_dyn.current_energy + total_flux;\n            elem_write.total_flux = elem_dyn.total_flux + std::abs(total_flux);\n        }\n        \n        // Swap buffers\n        std::swap(world.elements_dynamic, world.elements_dynamic_swap);\n    }\n}\n\n// Validate simulation results\nbool validateResults(const World& world) {\n    val_t energy_sum = 0.0;\n    val_t flux_sum = 0.0;\n    val_t energy_max = std::numeric_limits<val_t>::lowest();\n    val_t energy_min = std::numeric_limits<val_t>::max();\n    \n    for (const auto& elem : world.elements_dynamic) {\n        energy_sum += elem.current_energy;\n        flux_sum += elem.total_flux;\n        energy_max = std::max(elem.current_energy, energy_max);\n        energy_min = std::min(elem.current_energy, energy_min);\n    }\n    \n    printf(\"Validation results:\\n\");\n    printf(\"  Energy sum: %.12f\\n\", energy_sum);\n    printf(\"  Flux sum: %.2f\\n\", flux_sum);\n    printf(\"  Energy range: [%.6f, %.6f]\\n\", energy_min, energy_max);\n    \n    // Check for numerical issues\n    constexpr val_t energy_epsilon = 1e-8;\n    \n    if (!std::isfinite(energy_sum)) {\n        printf(\"  ERROR: Energy sum is not finite\\n\");\n        return false;\n    }\n    \n    if (std::abs(energy_sum) > energy_epsilon) {\n        printf(\"  WARNING: Energy sum diverged from 0 (expected conservation)\\n\");\n        // Don't fail validation as this can happen with external flows\n    }\n    \n    if (!std::isfinite(flux_sum)) {\n        printf(\"  ERROR: Flux sum is not finite\\n\");\n        return false;\n    }\n PASSED\\n\");\n    return true;\n}\n\n// Compute a simple hash of the results for verification\nuint64_t computeHash(const std::vector<ElementDynamic>& elements) {\n    uint64_t hash = 0;\n    for    }\n    return hash;\n}\n\nvoid printUsage(const char* progName) {\n    printf(\"Usage: %s [options]\\n\", progName);\n    printf(\"Options:\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -n <num>     Grid size (NxN Print results for external validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -h           Show this help message\\n\");\n}\n\nint main(int argc, char** argv) {\n    int n_elems_root = 512;\n    int n_iters = 10;\n  // Energy transfer coefficient\n    val_t external_flow;   // External energy source/sink\n};\n\n// Static connectivity information for each element\nstruct ElementStatic {\n    idx_t material_idx;\n    idx_t num_connections;\n    idx_t connected_idx[MAX_CONNECTIONS];     // Indices of connected elements\n    val_t connected_flux[MAX_CONNECTIONS];    // Flux coefficients for each connection\n};\n\n// Dynamic state for each element\nstruct ElementDynamic {\n    val_t current_energy;\n    val_t total_flux;\n};\n\n// World state\nstruct World {\n    std::vector<Material> materials;\n    std::vector<ElementStatic> elements_static;\n    std::vector<ElementDynamic> elements_dynamic;\n    std::vector<ElementDynamic> elements_dynamic_swap;\n};\n\n// Material type IDs\nconstexpr idx_t DEFAULT_MAT_ID = 0;\nconstexpr idx_t INFLOW_MAT_ID = 1;\nconstexpr idx_t OUTFLOW_MAT_ID = 2;\n\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// CUDA infrastructure\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\n#define CUDA_CHECK(call)                                                        \\\n    do {                                                                        \\\n        const cudaError_t err_ = (call);                                        \\\n        if (err_ != cudaSuccess) {                                              \\\n            fprintf(stderr, \"CUDA error %s:%d: %s\\n\", __FILE__, __LINE__,       \\\n                    cudaGetErrorString(err_));                                  \\\n            exit(EXIT_FAILURE);                                                 \\\n        }                                                                       \\\n    } while (0)\n\n// Threads per block for the update kernel\nconstexpr int BLOCK_SIZE = 256;\n\n// Device-side mesh in structure-of-arrays form.\n//\n// The host representation (array of ElementStatic) is convenient to build but\n// gives strided, cache-hostile access on a GPU: every thread would touch a\n// 144-byte record. On the device the same information is stored as separate\n// arrays, with the per-connection data transposed so that connection j of all\n// elements is contiguous (conn[j * n + i]). Consecutive threads therefore read\n// consecutive addresses for every load, which is what the memory system wants.\nstruct DeviceMesh {\n    uint32_t n_elems = 0;\n    uint32_t max_conn = 0;              // Largest num_connections in the mesh\n\n    Material* materials = nullptr;      // [n_materials]\n    uint32_t* material_idx = nullptr;   // [n_elems]\n    uint8_t* num_conn = nullptr;        // [n_elems]\n    uint32_t* conn_idx = nullptr;       // [max_conn * n_elems], transposed\n    val_t* conn_flux = nullptr;         // [max_conn * n_elems], transposed\n\n    val_t* energy[2] = {nullptr, nullptr};  // Ping-pong energy buffers\n    val_t* flux = nullptr;                  // Accumulated |flux| per element\n};\n\n// Compute energy flux between two elements\n__host__ __device__ inline val_t computeFlux(const Material& mat, val_t this_energy,\n                                             val_t connection_flux, val_t other_energy) {\n    return (other_energy - this_energy) *\n           mat.transfer_coeff * connection_flux * 0.25;\n}\n\n// One simulation step: one thread per element, grid-stride so that any mesh\n// size maps onto any launch configuration.\n//\n// The accumulated flux is updated in place: a thread only ever touches its own\n// element for that quantity, so no second buffer (and no extra traffic) is\n// needed. Energy is double-buffered because neighbours read it.\n__global__ __launch_bounds__(BLOCK_SIZE) void updateElementsKernel(\n    const uint32_t n_elems,\n    const val_t* __restrict__ energy_in,\n    val_t* __restrict__ energy_out,\n    val_t* __restrict__ flux_acc,\n    const uint32_t* __restrict__ conn_idx,\n    const val_t* __restrict__ conn_flux,\n    const uint8_t* __restrict__ num_conn,\n    const uint32_t* __restrict__ material_idx,\n    const Material* __restrict__ materials) {\n    const uint32_t stride = blockDim.x * gridDim.x;\n\n    for (uint32_t i = blockIdx.x * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x; i < n_elems; i += stride) {\n        const Material mat = materials[material_idx[i]];\n        const val_t energy = energy_in[i];\n\n        // Start with external flow\n        val_t total_flux = mat.external_flow;\n\n        // Add flux from all connected elements (same order as the reference\n        // implementation, so the floating-point result is bit-identical)\n        const uint32_t nc = num_conn[i];\n        for (uint32_t j = 0; j < nc; ++j) {\n            const uint32_t base = j * n_elems + i;\n            const uint32_t neighbor_idx = conn_idx[base];\n            total_flux += computeFlux(mat, energy, conn_flux[base],\n                                      __ldg(&energy_in[neighbor_idx]));\n        }\n\n        // Update element state\n        energy_out[i] = energy + total_flux;\n        flux_acc[i] += fabs(total_flux);\n    }\n}\n\n// Build a 2D square grid as an unstructured mesh\n// This represents computation on arbitrarily-shaped geometries\nvoid buildSquare2D(World& world, const int n_elems_root) {\n    const int n_elems = n_elems_root * n_elems_root;\n\n    // Initialize materials\n    world.materials.emplace_back(Material{0.8, 0.0});    // Default material\n    world.materials.emplace_back(Material{0.8, 0.5});    // Inflow material\n    world.materials.emplace_back(Material{0.8, -0.5});   // Outflow material\n\n    // Allocate elements\n    world.elements_static.resize(n_elems);\n    world.elements_dynamic.resize(n_elems);\n    world.elements_dynamic_swap.resize(n_elems);\n\n    // Initialize all elements with default material and zero energy\n    for (int i = 0; i < n_elems; ++i) {\n        world.elements_static[i].material_idx = DEFAULT_MAT_ID;\n        world.elements_static[i].num_connections = 0;\n        world.elements_dynamic[i].current_energy = 0.0;\n        world.elements_dynamic[i].total_flux = 0.0;\n    }\n\n    // Build connectivity: each element connects to its neighbors in 2D grid\n    for (int x = 0; x < n_elems_root; ++x) {\n        for (int y = 0; y < n_elems_root; ++y) {\n            const int idx = x * n_elems_root + y;\n            ElementStatic& elem = world.elements_static[idx];\n\n            // Connect to neighbors (up, down, left, right)\n            const int offsets[4][2] = {{1, 0}, {-1, 0}, {0, 1}, {0, -1}};\n\n            for (int n = 0; n < 4; ++n) {\n                const int nx = x + offsets[n][0];\n                const int ny = y + offsets[n][1];\n\n                // Check if neighbor is within bounds\n                if (nx >= 0 && nx < n_elems_root && ny >= 0 && ny < n_elems_root) {\n                    const int neighbor_idx = nx * n_elems_root + ny;\n                    elem.connected_idx[elem.num_connections] = neighbor_idx;\n                    elem.connected_flux[elem.num_connections] = 1.0;\n                    elem.num_connections++;\n                }\n            }\n        }\n    }\n\n    // Set corner elements as inflow/outflow to create interesting dynamics\n    const int last = n_elems_root - 1;\n    world.elements_static[0 * n_elems_root + 0].material_idx = INFLOW_MAT_ID;\n    world.elements_static[0 * n_elems_root + last].material_idx = OUTFLOW_MAT_ID;\n    world.elements_static[last * n_elems_root + 0].material_idx = OUTFLOW_MAT_ID;\n    world.elements_static[last * n_elems_root + last].material_idx = INFLOW_MAT_ID;\n}\n\n// Transfer the static mesh (connectivity, materials) to the device.\n// This is part of problem setup, like building the mesh itself, and is\n// therefore reported separately from the simulation time.\nvoid uploadMesh(const World& world, DeviceMesh& mesh) {\n    const size_t n_elems = world.elements_static.size();\n    if (n_elems > std::numeric_limits<uint32_t>::max()) {\n        fprintf(stderr, \"Mesh too large for 32-bit element indices\\n\");\n        exit(EXIT_FAILURE);\n    }\n    mesh.n_elems = static_cast<uint32_t>(n_elems);\n\n    // Repack connectivity into transposed structure-of-arrays form\n    size_t max_conn = 1;\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < n_elems; ++i) {\n        max_conn = std::max<size_t>(max_conn, world.elements_static[i].num_connections);\n    }\n    mesh.max_conn = static_cast<uint32_t>(max_conn);\n\n    std::vector<uint32_t> h_material_idx(n_elems);\n    std::vector<uint8_t> h_num_conn(n_elems);\n    std::vector<uint32_t> h_conn_idx(max_conn * n_elems, 0);\n    std::vector<val_t> h_conn_flux(max_conn * n_elems, 0.0);\n\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < n_elems; ++i) {\n        const ElementStatic& elem = world.elements_static[i];\n        h_material_idx[i] = static_cast<uint32_t>(elem.material_idx);\n        h_num_conn[i] = static_cast<uint8_t>(elem.num_connections);\n        for (idx_t j = 0; j < elem.num_connections; ++j) {\n            h_conn_idx[j * n_elems + i] = static_cast<uint32_t>(elem.connected_idx[j]);\n            h_conn_flux[j * n_elems + i] = elem.connected_flux[j];\n        }\n    }\n\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&mesh.materials, world.materials.size() * sizeof(Material)));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&mesh.material_idx, n_elems * sizeof(uint32_t)));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&mesh.num_conn, n_elems * sizeof(uint8_t)));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&mesh.conn_idx,  // Energy transfer coefficient\n    val_t external_flow;   // External energy source/sink\n};\n\n// Static connectivity information for each element\nstruct ElementStatic {\n    idx_t material_idx;\n    idx_t num_connections;\n    idx_t connected_idx[MAX_CONNECTIONS];     // Indices of connected elements\n    val_t connected_flux[MAX_CONNECTIONS];    // Flux coefficients for each connection\n};\n\n// Dynamic state for each element\nstruct ElementDynamic {\n    val_t current_energy;\n    val_t total_flux;\n};\n\n// World state\nstruct World {\n    std::vector<Material> materials;\n    std::vector<ElementStatic> elements_static;\n    std::vector<ElementDynamic> elements_dynamic;\n    std::vector<ElementDynamic> elements_dynamic_swap;\n};\n\n// Material type IDs\nconstexpr idx_t DEFAULT_MAT_ID = 0;\nconstexpr idx_t INFLOW_MAT_ID = 1;\nconstexpr idx_t OUTFLOW_MAT_ID = 2;\n\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// CUDA infrastructure\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\n#define CUDA_CHECK(call)                                                        \\\n    do {                                                                        \\\n        const cudaError_t err_ = (call);                                        \\\n        if (err_ != cudaSuccess) {                                              \\\n            fprintf(stderr, \"CUDA error %s:%d: %s\\n\", __FILE__, __LINE__,       \\\n                    cudaGetErrorString(err_));                                  \\\n            exit(EXIT_FAILURE);                                                 \\\n        }                                                                       \\\n    } while (0)\n\n// Threads per block for the update kernel\nconstexpr int BLOCK_SIZE = 256;\n\n// Device-side mesh in structure-of-arrays form.\n//\n// The host representation (array of ElementStatic) is convenient to build but\n// gives strided, cache-hostile access on a GPU: every thread would touch a\n// 144-byte record. On the device the same information is stored as separate\n// arrays, with the per-connection data transposed so that connection j of all\n// elements is contiguous (conn[j * n + i]). Consecutive threads therefore read\n// consecutive addresses for every load, which is what the memory system wants.\nstruct DeviceMesh {\n    uint32_t n_elems = 0;\n    uint32_t max_conn = 0;              // Largest num_connections in the mesh\n\n    Material* materials = nullptr;      // [n_materials]\n    uint32_t* material_idx = nullptr;   // [n_elems]\n    uint8_t* num_conn = nullptr;        // [n_elems]\n    uint32_t* conn_idx = nullptr;       // [max_conn * n_elems], transposed\n    val_t* conn_flux = nullptr;         // [max_conn * n_elems], transposed\n\n    val_t* energy[2] = {nullptr, nullptr};  // Ping-pong energy buffers\n    val_t* flux = nullptr;                  // Accumulated |flux| per element\n};\n\n// Compute energy flux between two elements\n__host__ __device__ inline val_t computeFlux(const Material& mat, val_t this_energy,\n                                             val_t connection_flux, val_t other_energy) {\n    return (other_energy - this_energy) *\n           mat.transfer_coeff * connection_flux * 0.25;\n}\n\n// One simulation step: one thread per element, grid-stride so that any mesh\n// size maps onto any launch configuration.\n//\n// The accumulated flux is updated in place: a thread only ever touches its own\n// element for that quantity, so no second buffer (and no extra traffic) is\n// needed. Energy is double-buffered because neighbours read it.\n__global__ __launch_bounds__(BLOCK_SIZE) void updateElementsKernel(\n    const uint32_t n_elems,\n    const val_t* __restrict__ energy_in,\n    val_t* __restrict__ energy_out,\n    val_t* __restrict__ flux_acc,\n    const uint32_t* __restrict__ conn_idx,\n    const val_t* __restrict__ conn_flux,\n    const uint8_t* __restrict__ num_conn,\n    const uint32_t* __restrict__ material_idx,\n    const Material* __restrict__ materials) {\n    const uint32_t stride = blockDim.x * gridDim.x;\n\n    for (uint32_t i = blockIdx.x * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x; i < n_elems; i += stride) {\n        const Material mat = materials[material_idx[i]];\n        const val_t energy = energy_in[i];\n\n        // Start with external flow\n        val_t total_flux = mat.external_flow;\n\n        // Add flux from all connected elements (same order as the reference\n        // implementation, so the floating-point result is bit-identical)\n        const uint32_t nc = num_conn[i];\n        for (uint32_t j = 0; j < nc; ++j) {\n            const uint32_t base = j * n_elems + i;\n            const uint32_t neighbor_idx = conn_idx[base];\n            total_flux += computeFlux(mat, energy, conn_flux[base],\n                                      __ldg(&energy_in[neighbor_idx]));\n        }\n\n        // Update element state\n        energy_out[i] = energy + total_flux;\n        flux_acc[i] += fabs(total_flux);\n    }\n}\n\n// Build a 2D square grid as an unstructured mesh\n// This represents computation on arbitrarily-shaped geometries\nvoid buildSquare2D(World& world, const int n_elems_root) {\n    const int n_elems = n_elems_root * n_elems_root;\n\n    // Initialize materials\n    world.materials.emplace_back(Material{0.8, 0.0});    // Default material\n    world.materials.emplace_back(Material{0.8, 0.5});    // Inflow material\n    world.materials.emplace_back(Material{0.8, -0.5});   // Outflow material\n\n    // Allocate elements\n    world.elements_static.resize(n_elems);\n    world.elements_dynamic.resize(n_elems);\n    world.elements_dynamic_swap.resize(n_elems);\n\n    // Initialize all elements with default material and zero energy\n    for (int i = 0; i < n_elems; ++i) {\n        world.elements_static[i].material_idx = DEFAULT_MAT_ID;\n        world.elements_static[i].num_connections = 0;\n        world.elements_dynamic[i].current_energy = 0.0;\n        world.elements_dynamic[i].total_flux = 0.0;\n    }\n\n    // Build connectivity: each element connects to its neighbors in 2D grid\n    for (int x = 0; x < n_elems_root; ++x) {\n        for (int y = 0; y < n_elems_root; ++y) {\n            const int idx = x * n_elems_root + y;\n            ElementStatic& elem = world.elements_static[idx];\n\n            // Connect to neighbors (up, down, left, right)\n            const int offsets[4][2] = {{1, 0}, {-1, 0}, {0, 1}, {0, -1}};\n\n            for (int n = 0; n < 4; ++n) {\n                const int nx = x + offsets[n][0];\n                const int ny = y + offsets[n][1];\n\n                // Check if neighbor is within bounds\n                if (nx >= 0 && nx < n_elems_root && ny >= 0 && ny < n_elems_root) {\n                    const int neighbor_idx = nx * n_elems_root + ny;\n                    elem.connected_idx[elem.num_connections] = neighbor_idx;\n                    elem.connected_flux[elem.num_connections] = 1.0;\n                    elem.num_connections++;\n                }\n            }\n        }\n    }\n\n    // Set corner elements as inflow/outflow to create interesting dynamics\n    const int last = n_elems_root - 1;\n    world.elements_static[0 * n_elems_root + 0].material_idx = INFLOW_MAT_ID;\n    world.elements_static[0 * n_elems_root + last].material_idx = OUTFLOW_MAT_ID;\n    world.elements_static[last * n_elems_root + 0].material_idx = OUTFLOW_MAT_ID;\n    world.elements_static[last * n_elems_root + last].material_idx = INFLOW_MAT_ID;\n}\n\n// Transfer the static mesh (connectivity, materials) to the device.\n// This is part of problem setup, like building the mesh itself, and is\n// therefore reported separately from the simulation time.\nvoid uploadMesh(const World& world, DeviceMesh& mesh) {\n    const size_t n_elems = world.elements_static.size();\n    if (n_elems > std::numeric_limits<uint32_t>::max()) {\n        fprintf(stderr, \"Mesh too large for 32-bit element indices\\n\");\n        exit(EXIT_FAILURE);\n    }\n    mesh.n_elems = static_cast<uint32_t>(n_elems);\n\n    // Repack connectivity into transposed structure-of-arrays form\n    size_t max_conn = 1;\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < n_elems; ++i) {\n        max_conn = n_elems; ++i) {\n        const ElementStatic& elem = world.elements_static[i];\n        h_material_idx[i] = static_cast<uint32_t>(elem.material_idx);\n        h_num_conn[i] = static_cast<uint8_t>(elem.num_connections);\n        for (idx_t j = 0; j < elem.num_connections; ++j) {\n            h_conn_idx[j * n_elems + i] = static_cast<uint32_t>(elem.connected_idx[j]);\n            h_conn_flux[j * n_elems + i] = elem.connected_flux[j];\n        }\n    }\n\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&mesh.materials, world.materials.size() * sizeof(Material)));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&mesh.material_idx, n_elems * sizeof(uint32_t)));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&mesh.num_conn, n_elems * sizeof(uint8_t)));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&mesh.conn_idx, h_conn_idx.size() * sizeof(uint32_t)));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&mesh.conn_flux, h_conn_flux.size() * sizeof(val_t)));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&mesh.energy[0], n_elems * sizeof(val_t)));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&mesh.energy[1], n_elems * sizeof(val_t)));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&mesh.flux, n_elems * sizeof(val_t)));\n\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(mesh.materials, world.materials.data(),\n                          world.materials.size() * sizeof(Material), cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(mesh.material_idx, h_material_idx.data(),\n                          n_elems * sizeof(uint32_t), cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(mesh.num_conn, h_num_conn.data(),\n                          n_elems * sizeof(uint8_t), cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(mesh.conn_idx, h_conn_idx.data(),\n                          h_conn_idx.size() * sizeof(uint32_t), cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(mesh.conn_flux, h_conn_flux.data(),\n                          h_conn_flux.size() * sizeof(val_t), cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));\n}\n\nvoid freeMesh(DeviceMesh& mesh) {\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(mesh.materials));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(mesh.material_idx));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(mesh.num_conn));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(mesh.conn_idx));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(mesh.conn_flux));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(mesh.energy[0]));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(mesh.energy[1]));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(mesh.flux));\n    mesh = DeviceMesh{};\n}\n\n// Run simulation for n_iters iterations on the GPU\nvoid runSimulation(World& world, DeviceMesh& mesh, const int n_iters) {\n    const uint32_t n_elems = mesh.n_elems;\n\n    // Upload the initial dynamic <vector>\n\n#include \"../common/results_output.hpp\"\n\n// Types to represent unstructured mesh elements\nusing idx_t = uint64_t;\nusing val_t = double;\n\n// Maximum number of connections per element (for a 2D grid: 4 neighbors)\nconstexpr int MAX_CONNECTIONS = 8;\n\n// Material properties for energy transfer\nstruct Material {\n    val_t transfer_coeff;  // Energy transfer coefficient\n    val_t external_flow;   // External energy source/sink\n};\n\n// Static connectivity information for each element\nstruct ElementStatic {\n    idx_t material_idx;\n    idx_t num_connections;\n    idx_t connected_idx[MAX_CONNECTIONS];     // Indices of connected elements\n    val_t connected_flux[MAX_CONNECTIONS];    // Flux coefficients for each connection\n};\n\n// Dynamic state for each element\nstruct ElementDynamic {\n    val_t current_energy;\n    val_t total_flux;\n};\n\n// World state\nstruct World {\n    std::vector<Material> materials;\n    std::vector<ElementStatic> elements_static;\n    std::vector<ElementDynamic> elements_dynamic;\n    std::vector<ElementDynamic> elements_dynamic_swap;\n};\n\n// Material type IDs\nconstexpr idx_t DEFAULT_MAT_ID = 0;\nconstexpr idx_t INFLOW_MAT_ID = 1;\nconstexpr idx_t OUTFLOW_MAT_ID = 2;\n\n// Build a 2D square grid as an unstructured mesh\n// This represents computation on arbitrarily-shaped geometries\nvoid buildSquare2D(World& world, const int n_elems_root) {\n    const int n_elems = n_elems_root * n_elems_root;\n    \n    // Initialize materials\n    world.materials.emplace_back(Material{0.8, 0.0});    // Default material\n    world.materials.emplace_back(Material{0.8, 0.5});    // Inflow material\n    world.materials.emplace_back(Material{0.8, -0.5});   // Outflow material\n    \n    // Allocate elements\n    world.elements_static.resize(n_elems);\n    world.elements_dynamic.resize(n_elems);\n    world.elements_dynamic_swap.resize(n_elems);\n    \n    // Initialize all elements with default material and zero energy\n    for (int i = 0; i < n_elems; ++i) {\n        world.elements_static[i].material_idx = DEFAULT_MAT_ID;\n        world.elements_static[i].num_connections = 0;\n        world.elements_dynamic[i].current_energy = 0.0;\n        world.elements_dynamic[i].total_flux = 0.0;\n    }\n    \n    // Build connectivity: each element connects to its neighbors in 2D grid\n    for (int x = 0; x < n_elems_root; ++x) {\n        for (int y = 0; y < n_elems_root; ++y) {\n            const int idx = x * n_elems_root + y;\n            ElementStatic& elem = world.elements_static[idx];\n            \n            // Connect to neighbors (up, down, left, right)\n            const int offsets[4][2] = {{1, 0}, {-1, 0}, {0, 1}, {0, -1}};\n            \n            for (int n = 0; n < 4; ++n) {\n                const int nx = x + offsets[n][0];\n                const int ny = y + offsets[n][1];\n                \n                // Check if neighbor is within bounds\n                if (nx >= 0 && nx < n_elems_root && ny >= 0 && ny < n_elems_root) {\n                    const int neighbor_idx = nx * n_elems_root + ny;\n                    elem.connected_idx[elem.num_connections] = neighbor_idx;\n                    elem.connected_flux[elem.num_connections] = 1.0;\n                    elem.num_connections++;\n                }\n            }\n        }\n    }\n    \n    // Set corner elements as inflow/outflow to create interesting dynamics\n    const int last = n_elems_root - 1;\n    world.elements_static[0 * n_elems_root +    world.elements_static[last * n_elems_root + last].material_idx = INFLOW_MAT_ID;\n}\n\n// Compute energy flux between two elements\ninline val_t computeFlux(const Material& mat, const ElementDynamic& this_elem,\n                        val_t connection_flux, const ElementDynamic& other_elem) {\n    return (other_elem.current_energy - this_elem.current_energy) * \n           mat.transfer_coeff * connection_flux * 0.25;\n}\n\n// Run simulation for n_iters iterations\nvoid runSimulation(World& world, const int n_iters) {\n    const size_t n_elems = world.elements_static.size();\n    \n    for (int iter = 0; iter < n_iters; ++iter) {\n        // Update all elements\n        for (size_t i = 0; i < n_elems; ++i) {\n            const ElementStatic& elem_static = world.elements_static[i];\n            const ElementDynamic& elem_dyn = world.elements_dynamic[i];\n            const Material& mat = world.materials[elem_static.material_idx];\n            \n            // Start with external flow\n            val_t total_flux = mat.external_flow;\n            \n            // Add flux from all connected elements\n            for (idx_t j = 0; j < elem_static.num_connections; ++j) {\n                const idx_t neighbor_idx = elem_static.connected_idx[j];\n                const ElementDynamic& neighbor_dyn = world.elements_dynamic[neighbor_idx];\n                total_flux += computeFlux(mat, elem_dyn, elem_static.connected_flux[j], neighbor_dyn);\n            }\n            \n            // Update element state\n            ElementDynamic& elem_write = world.elements_dynamic_swap[i];\n            elem_write.current_energy = elem_dyn.current_energy + total_flux;\n            elem_write.total_flux = elem_dyn.total_flux + std::abs(total_flux);\n        }\n        \n        // Swap buffers\n        std::swap(world.elements_dynamic, world.elements_dynamic_swap);\n    }\n}\n\n// Validate simulation results\nbool validateResults(const World& world) {\n    val_t energy_sum = 0.0;\n    val_t flux_sum = 0.0;\n    val_t energy_max = std::numeric_limits<val_t>::lowest();\n    val_t energy_min = std::numeric_limits<val_t>::max();\n    \n    for (const auto& elem : world.elements_dynamic) {\n        energy_sum += elem.current_energy;\n        flux_sum += elem.total_flux;\n        energy_max = std::max(elem.current_energy, energy_max);\n        energy_min = std::min(elem.current_energy, energy_min);\n    }\n    \n    printf(\"Validation results:\\n\");\n    printf(\"  Energy sum: %.12f\\n\", energy_sum);\n    printf(\"  Flux sum: %.2f\\n\", flux_sum);\n    printf(\"  Energy range: [%.6f, %.6f]\\n\", energy_min, energy_max);\n    \n    // Check for numerical issues\n    constexpr val_t energy_epsilon = 1e-8;\n    \n    if (!std::isfinite(energy_sum)) {\n        printf(\"  ERROR: Energy sum is not finite\\n\");\n        return false;\n    }\n    \n    if (std::abs(energy_sum) > energy_epsilon) {\n        printf(\"  WARNING: Energy sum diverged from 0 (expected conservation)\\n\");\n        // Don't fail validation as this can happen with external flows\n    }\n    \n    if (!std::isfinite(flux_sum)) {\n        printf(\"  ERROR: Flux sum is not finite\\n\");\n        return false;\n    }\n    \n    if (!std::isfinite(energy_max) || !std::isfinite(energy_min)) {\n        printf(\"  ERROR: Energy extrema are not finite\\n\");\n        return false;\n    }\n    \n    printf(\"  Validation: PASSED\\n\");\n    return true;\n}\n\n// Compute a simple hash of the results for verification\nuint64_t computeHash(const std::vector<ElementDynamic>& elements) {\n    uint64_t hash = 0;\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < elements.size(); ++i) {\n        // Simple hash combining energy and flux values\n        const uint64_t* e_ptr = reinterpret_cast<const uint64_t*>(&elements[i].current_energy);\n    }\n    return hash;\n}\n\nvoid printUsage(const char* progName) {\n    printf(\"Usage: %s [options]\\n\", progName);\n    printf(\"Options:\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -n <num>     Grid size (NxN elements) (default: 512)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -i <num>     Number of simulation iterations (default: 10)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -v           Enable validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -r           Print results for external validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -h           Show this help message\\n\");\n}\n\nint main(int argc, char** argv) {\n    int n_elems_root = 512;\n    int n_iters = 10;\n    bool validate = false;\n    bool printResults = false;\n    \n    // Parse command line arguments\n    for (int i = 1; i < argc; ++i) {\n        if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-n\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n // Calculate memory usage\n    const size_t static_mem = world.elements_static.size() * sizeof(ElementStatic);\n    const size_t dynamic_mem = world.elements_dynamic.size() * sizeof(ElementDynamic) * 2;\n    const size_t total_mem = static_mem + dynamic_mem;\n    printf(\"Memory usage: %.2f MB (static: %.2f MB, dynamic: %.2f MB)\\n\",\n           total_mem / (1024.0 * 1024.0),\n  // Energy transfer coefficient\n    val_t external_flow;   // External energy source/sink\n};\n\n// Static connectivity information for each element\nstruct ElementStatic {\n    idx_t material_idx;\n    idx_t num_connections;\n    idx_t connected_idx[MAX_CONNECTIONS];     // Indices of connected elements\n    val_t connected_flux[MAX_CONNECTIONS];    // Flux coefficients for each connection\n};\n\n// Dynamic state for each element\nstruct ElementDynamic {\n    val_t current_energy;\n    val_t total_flux;\n};\n\n// World state\nstruct World {\n    std::vector<Material> materials;\n    std::vector<ElementStatic> elements_static;\n    std::vector<ElementDynamic> elements_dynamic;\n    std::vector<ElementDynamic> elements_dynamic_swap;\n};\n\n// Material type IDs\nconstexpr idx_t DEFAULT_MAT_ID = 0;\nconstexpr idx_t INFLOW_MAT_ID = 1;\nconstexpr idx_t OUTFLOW_MAT_ID = 2;\n\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// CUDA infrastructure\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\n#define CUDA_CHECK(call)                                                        \\\n    do {                                                                        \\\n        const cudaError_t err_ = (call);                                        \\\n        if (err_ != cudaSuccess) {                                              \\\n            fprintf(stderr, \"CUDA error %s:%d: %s\\n\", __FILE__, __LINE__,       \\\n                    cudaGetErrorString(err_));                                  \\\n            exit(EXIT_FAILURE);                                                 \\\n        }                                                                       \\\n    } while (0)\n\n// Threads per block for the update kernel\nconstexpr int BLOCK_SIZE = 256;\n\n// Device-side mesh in structure-of-arrays form.\n//\n// The host representation (array of ElementStatic) is convenient to build but\n// gives strided, cache-hostile access on a GPU: every thread would touch a\n// 144-byte record. On the device the same information is stored as separate\n// arrays, with the per-connection data transposed so that connection j of all\n// elements is contiguous (conn[j * n + i]). Consecutive threads therefore read\n// consecutive addresses for every load, which is what the memory system wants.\nstruct DeviceMesh {\n    uint32_t n_elems = 0;\n    uint32_t max_conn = 0;              // Largest num_connections in the mesh\n\n    Material* materials = nullptr;      // [n_materials]\n    uint32_t* material_idx = nullptr;   // [n_elems]\n    uint8_t* num_conn = nullptr;        // [n_elems]\n    uint32_t* conn_idx = nullptr;       // [max_conn * n_elems], transposed\n    val_t* conn_flux = nullptr;         // [max_conn * n_elems], transposed\n\n    val_t* energy[2] = {nullptr, nullptr};  // Ping-pong energy buffers\n    val_t* flux = nullptr;                  // Accumulated |flux| per element\n};\n\n// Compute energy flux between two elements\n__host__ __device__ inline val_t computeFlux(const Material& mat, val_t this_energy,\n                                             val_t connection_flux, val_t other_energy) {\n    return (other_energy - this_energy) *\n           mat.transfer_coeff * connection_flux * 0.25;\n}\n\n// One simulation step: one thread per element, grid-stride so that any mesh\n// size maps onto any launch configuration.\n//\n// The accumulated flux is updated in place: a thread only ever touches its own\n// element for that quantity, so no second buffer (and no extra traffic) is\n// needed. Energy is double-buffered because neighbours read it.\n__global__ __launch_bounds__(BLOCK_SIZE) void updateElementsKernel(\n    const uint32_t n_elems,\n    const val_t* __restrict__ energy_in,\n    val_t* __restrict__ energy_out,\n    val_t* __restrict__ flux_acc,\n    const uint32_t* __restrict__ conn_idx,\n    const val_t* __restrict__ conn_flux,\n    const uint8_t* __restrict__ num_conn,\n    const uint32_t* __restrict__ material_idx,\n    const Material* __restrict__ materials) {\n    const uint32_t stride = blockDim.x * gridDim.x;\n\n    for (uint32_t i = blockIdx.x * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x; i < n_elems; i += stride) {\n        const Material mat = materials[material_idx[i]];\n        const val_t energy = energy_in[i];\n\n        // Start with external flow\n        val_t total_flux = mat.external_flow;\n\n        // Add flux from all connected elements (same order as the reference\n        // implementation, so the floating-point result is bit-identical)\n        const uint32_t nc = num_conn[i];\n        for (uint32_t j = 0; j < nc; ++j) {\n            const uint32_t base = j * n_elems + i;\n            const uint32_t neighbor_idx = conn_idx[base];\n            total_flux += computeFlux(mat, energy, conn_flux[base],\n                                      __ldg(&energy_in[neighbor_idx]));\n        }\n\n        // Update element state\n        energy_out[i] = energy + total_flux;\n        flux_acc[i] += fabs(total_flux);\n    }\n}\n\n// Build a 2D square grid as an unstructured mesh\n// This represents computation on arbitrarily-shaped geometries\nvoid buildSquare2D(World& world, const int n_elems_root) {\n    const int n_elems = n_elems_root * n_elems_root;\n\n    // Initialize materials\n    world.materials.emplace_back(Material{0.8, 0.0});    // Default material\n    world.materials.emplace_back(Material{0.8, 0.5});    // Inflow material\n    world.materials.emplace_back(Material{0.8, -0.5});   // Outflow material\n\n    // Allocate elements\n    world.elements_static.resize(n_elems);\n    world.elements_dynamic.resize(n_elems);\n    world.elements_dynamic_swap.resize(n_elems);\n\n    // Initialize all elements with default material and zero energy\n    for (int i = 0; i < n_elems; ++i) {\n        world.elements_static[i].material_idx = DEFAULT_MAT_ID;\n        world.elements_static[i].num_connections = 0;\n        world.elements_dynamic[i].current_energy = 0.0;\n        world.elements_dynamic[i].total_flux = 0.0;\n    }\n\n    // Build connectivity: each element connects to its neighbors in 2D grid\n    for (int x = 0; x < n_elems_root; ++x) {\n        for (int y = 0; y < n_elems_root; ++y) {\n            const int idx = x * n_elems_root + y;\n            ElementStatic& elem = world.elements_static[idx];\n\n            // Connect to neighbors (up, down, left, right)\n            const int offsets[4][2] = {{1, 0}, {-1, 0}, {0, 1}, {0, -1}};\n\n            for (int n = 0; n < 4; ++n) {\n                const int nx = x + offsets[n][0];\n                const int ny = y + offsets[n][1];\n\n                // Check if neighbor is within bounds\n                if (nx >= 0 && nx < n_elems_root && ny >= 0 && ny < n_elems_root) {\n                    const int neighbor_idx = nx * n_elems_root + ny;\n                    elem.connected_idx[elem.num_connections] = neighbor_idx;\n                    elem.connected_flux[elem.num_connections] = 1.0;\n                    elem.num_connections++;\n                }\n            }\n        }\n    }\n\n    // Set corner elements as inflow/outflow to create interesting dynamics\n    const int last = n_elems_root - 1;\n    world.elements_static[0 * n_elems_root + 0].material_idx = INFLOW_MAT_ID;\n    world.elements_static[0 * n_elems_root + last].material_idx = OUTFLOW_MAT_ID;\n    world.elements_static[last * n_elems_root + 0].material_idx = OUTFLOW_MAT_ID;\n    world.elements_static[last * n_elems_root + last].material_idx = INFLOW_MAT_ID;\n}\n\n// Transfer the static mesh (connectivity, materials) to the device.\n// This is part of problem setup, like building the mesh itself, and is\n// therefore reported separately from the simulation time.\nvoid uploadMesh(const World& world, DeviceMesh& mesh) {\n    const size_t n_elems = world.elements_static.size();\n    if (n_elems > std::numeric_limits<uint32_t>::max()) {\n        fprintf(stderr, \"Mesh too large for 32-bit element indices\\n\");\n        exit(EXIT_FAILURE);\n    }\n    mesh.n_elems = static_cast<uint32_t>(n_elems);\n\n    // Repack connectivity into transposed structure-of-arrays form\n    size_t max_conn = 1;\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < n_elems; ++i) {\n        max_conn = std::max<size_t>(max_conn, world.elements_static[i].num_connections);\n    }\n    mesh.max_conn = static_cast<uint32_t>(max_conn);\n\n    std::vector<uint32_t> h_material_idx(n_elems);\n    std::vector<uint8_t> h_num_conn(n_elems);\n    std::vector<uint32_t> h_conn_idx(max_conn * n_elems, 0);\n    std::vector<val_t> h_conn_flux(max_conn * n_elems, 0.0);\n\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < n_elems; ++i) {\n        const ElementStatic& elem = world.elements_static[i];\n        h_material_idx[i] = static_cast<uint32_t>(elem.material_idx);\n        h_num_conn[i] = static_cast<uint8_t>(elem.num_connections);\n        for (idx_t j = 0; j < elem.num_connections; ++j) {\n            h_conn_idx[j * n_elems + i] = static_cast<uint32_t>(elem.connected_idx[j]);\n            h_conn_flux[j * n_elems + i] = elem.connected_flux[j];\n        }\n    }\n\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&mesh.materials, world.materials.size() * sizeof(Material)));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&mesh.material_idx, n_elems * sizeof(uint32_t)));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&mesh.num_conn, n_elems * sizeof(uint8_t)));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&mesh.conn_idx, h_conn_idx.size() * sizeof(uint32_t)));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&mesh.conn_flux, h_conn_flux.size() * sizeof(val_t)));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&mesh.energy[0], n_elems * sizeof(val_t)));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&mesh.energy[1], n_elems * sizeof(val_t)));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&mesh.flux, n_elems * sizeof(val_t)));\n\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(mesh.materials, CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(mesh.conn_flux, h_conn_flux.data(),\n                          h_conn_flux.size() * sizeof(val_t), cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));\n}\n\nvoid freeMesh(DeviceMesh& mesh) {\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(mesh.materials));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(mesh.material_idx));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(mesh.num_conn));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(mesh.conn_idx));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(mesh.conn_flux));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(mesh.energy[0]));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(mesh.energy[1]));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(mesh.flux));\n    mesh = DeviceMesh{};\n}\n\n// Run simulation for n_iters iterations on the GPU\nvoid runSimulation(World& world, DeviceMesh& mesh, const int n_iters) {\n    const uint32_t n_elems = mesh.n_elems;\n\n    // Upload the initial dynamic state (energy / accumulated flux)\n    std::vector<val_t> h_energy(n_elems);\n    std::vector<val_t> h_flux(n_elems);\n    for (uint32_t i = 0; i < n_elems; ++i) {\n        h_energy[i] = validateResults(const World& world) {\n    val_t energy_sum = 0.0;\n    val_t flux_sum = 0.0;\n    val_t energy_max = std::numeric_limits<val_t>::lowest();\n    val_t energy_min = std::numeric_limits<val_t>::max();\n\n    for (const auto& elem : world.elements_dynamic) {\n        energy_sum += elem.current_energy;\n        flux_sum += elem.total_flux;\n        energy_max =  // Energy transfer coefficient\n    val_t external_flow;   // External energy source/sink\n};\n\n// Static connectivity information for each element\nstruct ElementStatic {\n    idx_t material_idx;\n    idx_t num_connections;\n    idx_t connected_idx[MAX_CONNECTIONS];     // Indices of connected elements\n    val_t connected_flux[MAX_CONNECTIONS];    // Flux coefficients for each connection\n};\n\n// Dynamic state for each element\nstruct ElementDynamic {\n    val_t current_energy;\n    val_t total_flux;\n};\n\n// World state\nstruct World {\n    std::vector<Material> materials;\n    std::vector<ElementStatic> elements_static;\n    std::vector<ElementDynamic> elements_dynamic;\n    std::vector<ElementDynamic> elements_dynamic_swap;\n};\n\n// Material type IDs\nconstexpr idx_t DEFAULT_MAT_ID = 0;\nconstexpr idx_t INFLOW_MAT_ID = 1;\nconstexpr idx_t OUTFLOW_MAT_ID = 2;\n\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// CUDA infrastructure\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\n#define CUDA_CHECK(call)                                                        \\\n    do {                                                                        \\\n        const cudaError_t err_ = (call);                                  \\\n            exit(EXIT_FAILURE);                                                 \\\n        }                                                                       \\\n    } while (0)\n\n// Threads per block for the update kernel\nconstexpr int BLOCK_SIZE = 256;\n\n// Device-side mesh in structure-of-arrays form.\n//\n// The host representation (array of ElementStatic) is convenient to build but\n// gives strided, cache-hostile access on a GPU: every thread would touch a\n// 144-byte record. On the device the same information is stored as separate\n// arrays, with the per-connection data transposed so that connection j of all\n// elements is contiguous (conn[j * n + i]). Consecutive threads therefore read\n// consecutive addresses for every load, which is what the memory system wants.\nstruct DeviceMesh {\n    uint32_t n_elems = 0;\n    uint32_t max_conn = 0;              // Largest num_connections in the mesh\n\n    Material* materials = nullptr;      // [n_materials]\n    uint32_t* material_idx = nullptr;   // [n_elems]\n    uint8_t* num_conn = nullptr;        // [n_elems]\n    uint32_t* conn_idx = nullptr;       // [max_conn * n_elems], transposed\n    val_t* conn_flux = nullptr;         // [max_conn * n_elems], transposed\n\n    val_t* energy[2] = {nullptr, nullptr};  // Ping-pong energy buffers\n    val_t* flux = nullptr;                  // Accumulated |flux| per element\n};\n\n// Compute energy flux between two elements\n__host__ __device__ inline val_t computeFlux(const Material& mat, val_t this_energy,\n                                             val_t connection_flux, val_t other_energy) {\n    return (other_energy - this_energy) *\n           mat.transfer_coeff * connection_flux * 0.25;\n}\n\n// One simulation step: one thread per element, grid-stride so that any mesh\n// size maps onto any launch configuration.\n//\n// The accumulated flux is updated in place: a thread only ever touches its own\n// element for that quantity, so no second buffer (and no extra traffic) is\n// needed. Energy is double-buffered because neighbours read it.\n__global__ __launch_bounds__(BLOCK_SIZE) void updateElementsKernel(\n    const uint32_t n_elems,\n    const val_t* __restrict__ energy_in,\n    val_t* __restrict__ energy_out,\n    val_t* __restrict__ flux_acc,\n    const uint32_t* __restrict__ conn_idx,\n    const val_t* __restrict__ conn_flux,\n    const uint8_t* __restrict__ num_conn,\n    const uint32_t* __restrict__ material_idx,\n    const Material* __restrict__ materials) {\n    const uint32_t stride = blockDim.x * gridDim.x;\n\n    for (uint32_t i = blockIdx.x * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x; i < n_elems; i += stride) {\n        const Material mat = materials[material_idx[i]];\n        const val_t energy = energy_in[i];\n\n        // Start with external flow\n        val_t total_flux = mat.external_flow;\n\n        // Add flux from all connected elements (same order as the reference\n        // implementation, so the floating-point result is bit-identical)\n        const uint32_t nc = num_conn[i];\n        for (uint32_t j = 0; j < nc; ++j) {\n            const uint32_t base = j * n_elems + i;\n            const uint32_t neighbor_idx = conn_idx[base];\n            total_flux += computeFlux(mat, energy, conn_flux[base],\n                                      __ldg(&energy_in[neighbor_idx]));\n        }\n\n        // Update element state\n        energy_out[i] = energy + total_flux;\n        flux_acc[i] += fabs(total_flux);\n    }\n}\n\n// Build a 2D square grid as an unstructured mesh\n// This represents computation on arbitrarily-shaped geometries\nvoid buildSquare2D(World& world, const int n_elems_root) {\n    const int n_elems = n_elems_root * n_elems_root;\n\n    // Initialize materials\n    world.materials.emplace_back(Material{0.8, 0.0});    // Default material\n    world.materials.emplace_back(Material{0.8, 0.5});    // Inflow material\n    world.materials.emplace_back(Material{0.8, -0.5});   // Outflow material\n\n    // Allocate elements\n    world.elements_static.resize(n_elems);\n    world.elements_dynamic.resize(n_elems);\n    world.elements_dynamic_swap.resize(n_elems);\n\n    // Initialize all elements with default material and zero energy\n    for (int i = 0; i < n_elems; ++i) {\n        world.elements_static[i].material_idx = DEFAULT_MAT_ID;\n        world.elements_static[i].num_connections = 0;\n        world.elements_dynamic[i].current_energy = 0.0;\n        world.elements_dynamic[i].total_flux = 0.0;\n    }\n\n    // Build connectivity: each element connects to its neighbors in 2D grid\n    for (int x = 0; x < n_elems_root; ++x) {\n        for (int y = 0; y < n_elems_root; ++y) {\n            const int idx = x * n_elems_root + y;\n            ElementStatic& elem = world.elements_static[idx];\n\n            // Connect to neighbors (up, down, left, right)\n            const int offsets[4][2] = {{1, 0}, {-1, 0}, {0, 1}, {0, -1}};\n\n            for (int n = 0; n < 4; ++n) {\n                const int nx = x + offsets[n][0];\n                const int ny = y + offsets[n][1];\n\n                // Check if neighbor is within bounds\n                if (nx >= 0 && nx < n_elems_root && ny >= 0 && ny < n_elems_root) {\n                    const int neighbor_idx = nx * n_elems_root + ny;\n                    elem.connected_idx[elem.num_connections] = neighbor_idx;\n                    elem.connected_flux[elem.num_connections] = 1.0;\n                    elem.num_connections++;\n                }\n            }\n        }\n    }\n\n    // Set corner elements as inflow/outflow to create interesting dynamics\n    const int last = n_elems_root - 1;\n    world.elements_static[0 * n_elems_root + 0].material_idx = INFLOW_MAT_ID;\n    world.elements_static[0 * n_elems_root + last].material_idx = OUTFLOW_MAT_ID;\n    world.elements_static[last * n_elems_root + 0].material_idx = OUTFLOW_MAT_ID;\n    world.elements_static[last * n_elems_root + last].material_idx = INFLOW_MAT_ID;\n}\n\n// Transfer the static mesh (connectivity, materials) to the device.\n// This is part of problem setup, like building the mesh itself, and is\n// therefore reported separately from the simulation time.\nvoid uploadMesh(const World& world, DeviceMesh& mesh) {\n    const size_t n_elems = world.elements_static.size();\n    if (n_elems > std::numeric_limits<uint32_t>::max()) {\n        fprintf(stderr, \"Mesh too large for 32-bit element indices\\n\");\n        exit(EXIT_FAILURE);\n    }\n    mesh.n_elems = static_cast<uint32_t>(n_elems);\n\n    // Repack connectivity into transposed structure-of-arrays form\n    size_t max_conn = 1;\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < n_elems; ++i) {\n        max_conn = std::max<size_t>(max_conn, world.elements_static[i].num_connections);\n    }\n    mesh.max_conn = static_cast<uint32_t>(max_conn);\n\n    std::vector<uint32_t> h_material_idx(n_elems);\n    std::vector<uint8_t> h_num_conn(n_elems);\n    std::vector<uint32_t> h_conn_idx(max_conn * n_elems, 0);\n    std::vector<val_t> h_conn_flux(max_conn * n_elems, 0.0);\n\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < n_elems; ++i) {\n        const ElementStatic& elem = world.elements_static[i];\n        h_material_idx[i] = static_cast<uint32_t>(elem.material_idx);\n        h_num_conn[i] = static_cast<uint8_t>(elem.num_connections);\n        for (idx_t j = 0; j < elem.num_connections; ++j) {\n            h_conn_idx[j * n_elems + i] = static_cast<uint32_t>(elem.connected_idx[j]);\n            h_conn_flux[j * n_elems + i] = elem.connected_flux[j];\n        }\n    }\n\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&mesh.materials, world.materials.size() * sizeof(Material)));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&mesh.material_idx, n_elems * sizeof(uint32_t)));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&mesh.num_conn, n_elems * sizeof(uint8_t)));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&mesh.conn_idx, h_conn_idx.size() * sizeof(uint32_t)));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&mesh.conn_flux, h_conn_flux.size() * sizeof(val_t)));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&mesh.energy[0], n_elems * sizeof(val_t)));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&mesh.energy[1], n_elems * sizeof(val_t)));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&mesh.flux, n_elems * sizeof(val_t)));\n\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(mesh.materials, world.materials.data(),\n                          world.materials.size() * sizeof(Material), cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(mesh.material_idx, h_material_idx.data(),\n                          n_elems * sizeof(uint32_t), cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(mesh.num_conn, h_num_conn.data(),\n                          n_elems * sizeof(uint8_t), cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(mesh.conn_idx, h_conn_idx.data(),\n                          h_conn_idx.size() * sizeof(uint32_t), cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(mesh.conn_flux, h_conn_flux.data(),\n                          h_conn_flux.size() * sizeof(val_t), cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));\n\n    // Warm up: force the kernel module to be loaded now, so that one-time\n    // runtime initialization is not attributed to the simulation. The launch\n    // itself is a no-op (zero elements).\n    updateElementsKernel<<<1, BLOCK_SIZE>>>(0, mesh.energy[0], mesh.energy[1], mesh.flux,\n                                            mesh.conn_idx, mesh.conn_flux, mesh.num_conn,\n                                            mesh.material_idx, mesh.materials);\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetLastError());\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaDeviceSynchronize());\n}\n\nvoid freeMesh(DeviceMesh& mesh) {\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(mesh.materials));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(mesh.material_idx));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(mesh.num_conn));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(mesh.conn_idx));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(mesh.conn_flux));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(mesh.energy[0]));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(mesh.energy[1]));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(mesh.flux));\n    mesh = DeviceMesh{};\n}\n\n// Run simulation for n_iters iterations on the GPU\nvoid runSimulation(World& world, DeviceMesh& mesh, const int n_iters) {\n    const uint32_t n_elems = mesh.n_elems;\n\n    // Upload the initial dynamic state (energy / accumulated flux)\n    std::vector<val_t> h_energy(n_elems);\n    std::vector<val_t> h_flux(n_elems);\n    for (uint32_t i = 0; i < n_elems; ++i) {\n        h_energy[i] = world.elements_dynamic[i].current_energy;\n        h_flux[i] = world.elements_dynamic[i].total_flux;\n    }\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(mesh.energy[0], h_energy.data(), n_elems * sizeof(val_t),\n                          cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(mesh.flux, h_flux.data(), n_elems * sizeof(val_t),\n                          cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));\n\n    const int blocks = static_cast<int>((n_elems + BLOCK_SIZE - 1) / BLOCK_SIZE);\n\n    int cur = 0;\n    for (int iter = 0; iter < n_iters; ++iter) {\n        updateElementsKernel<<<blocks, BLOCK_SIZE>>>(\n            n_elems, mesh.energy[cur], mesh.energy[cur ^ 1], mesh.flux,\n            mesh.conn_idx, mesh.conn_flux, mesh.num_conn,\n            mesh.material_idx, mesh.materials);\n        cur ^= 1;\n    }\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetLastError());\n\n    // Bring the final state back into the host representation\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(h_energy.data(), mesh.energy[cur], n_elems * sizeof(val_t),\n                          cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(h_flux.data(), mesh.flux, n_elems * sizeof(val_t),\n                          cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost));\n    for (uint32_t i = 0; i < n_elems; ++i) {\n        world.elements_dynamic[i].current_energy = h_energy[i];\n        world.elements_dynamic[i].total_flux = h_flux[i];\n    }\n}\n\n// Validate simulation results\nbool validateResults(const World& world) {\n    val_t energy_sum = 0.0;\n    val_t flux_sum = 0.0;\n    val_t energy_max = std::numeric_limits<val_t>::lowest();\n    val_t energy_min = std::numeric_limits<val_t>::max();\n\n    for (const auto& elem : world.elements_dynamic) {\n        energy_sum += elem.current_energy;\n        flux_sum += elem.total_flux;\n        energy_max = std::max(elem.current_energy, energy_max);\n        energy_min = std::min(elem.current_energy, energy_min);\n    }\n\n    printf(\"Validation results:\\n\");\n    printf(\"  Energy sum: %.12f\\n\", energy_sum);\n    printf(\"  Flux sum: %.2f\\n\", flux_sum);\n    printf(\"  Energy range: [%.6f, %.6f]\\n\", energy_min, energy_max);\n\n    // Check for numerical issues\n    constexpr val_t energy_epsilon = 1e-8;\n\n    if (!std::isfinite(energy_sum)) {\n        printf(\"  ERROR: Energy sum is not finite\\n\");\n        return false;\n    }\n\n    if (std::abs(energy_sum) > energy_epsilon) {\n        printf(\"  WARNING: Energy sum diverged from 0 (expected conservation)\\n\");\n        // Don't fail validation as this can happen with external flows\n    }\n\n    if (!std::isfinite(flux_sum)) {\n        printf(\"  ERROR: Flux sum is not finite\\n\");\n        return false;\n    }\n\n    if (!std::isfinite(energy_max) || !std::isfinite(energy_min)) {\n        printf(\"  ERROR: Energy extrema are not finite\\n\");\n        return false;\n    }\n\n    printf(\"  Validation: PASSED\\n\");\n    return true;\n}\n\n// Compute a simple hash of the results for verification\nuint64_t computeHash(const std::vector<ElementDynamic>& elements) {\n    uint64_t hash = 0;\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < elements.size(); ++i) {\n        // Simple hash combining energy and flux values\n        const uint64_t* e_ptr = reinterpret_cast<const uint64_t*>(&elements[i].current_energy);\n        const uint64_t* f_ptr = reinterpret_cast<const uint64_t*>(&elements[i].total_flux);\n        hash ^= (*e_ptr + i) * 0x9e3779b97f4a7c15ULL;\n        hash ^= (*f_ptr + i) * 0xbf58476d1ce4e5b9ULL;\n    }\n    return hash;\n}\n\nvoid printUsage(const char* progName) {\n    printf(\"Usage: %s [options]\\n\", progName);\n    printf(\"Options:\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -n <num>     Grid size (NxN elements) (default: 512)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -i <num>     Number of simulation iterations (default: 10)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -v           Enable validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -r           Print results for external validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -h           Show this help message\\n\");\n}\n\nint main(int argc, char** argv) {\n    int n_elems_root = 512;\n    int n_iters = 10;\n    bool validate = false;\n    bool printResults = false;\n\n    // Parse command line arguments\n    for (int i = 1; i < argc; ++i) {\n        if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-n\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            n_elems_root = atoi(argv[++i]);\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-i\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            n_iters = atoi(argv[++i]);\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-v\") == 0) {\n            validate = true;\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-r\") == 0) {\n            printResults = true;\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-h\") == 0) {\n            printUsage(argv[0]);\n            return 0;\n        } else {\n            printf(\"Unknown option: %s\\n\", argv[i]);\n            printUsage(argv[0]);\n            return 1;\n        }\n    }\n\n    const int n_elems = n_elems_root * n_elems_root;\n\n    printf(\"Unstructured Mesh Energy Transfer Benchmark\\n\");\n  // Energy transfer coefficient\n    val_t external_flow;   // External energy source/sink\n};\n\n// Static connectivity information for each element\nstruct ElementStatic {\n    idx_t material_idx;\n    idx_t num_connections;\n    idx_t connected_idx[MAX_CONNECTIONS];     // Indices of connected elements\n    val_t connected_flux[MAX_CONNECTIONS];    // Flux coefficients for each connection\n};\n\n// Dynamic state for each element\nstruct ElementDynamic {\n    val_t current_energy;\n    val_t total_flux;\n};\n\n// World state\nstruct World {\n    std::vector<Material> materials;\n    std::vector<ElementStatic> elements_static;\n    std::vector<ElementDynamic> elements_dynamic;\n    std::vector<ElementDynamic> elements_dynamic_swap;\n};\n\n// Material type IDs\nconstexpr idx_t DEFAULT_MAT_ID = 0;\nconstexpr idx_t INFLOW_MAT_ID = 1;\nconstexpr idx_t OUTFLOW_MAT_ID = 2;\n\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// CUDA infrastructure\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\n#define CUDA_CHECK(call)                                                        \\\n    do {                                                                        \\\n        const cudaError_t err_ = (call);                                        \\\n        if (err_ != cudaSuccess) {                                              \\\n            fprintf(stderr, \"CUDA error %s:%d: %s\\n\", __FILE__, __LINE__,       \\\n                    cudaGetErrorString(err_));                                  \\\n            exit(EXIT_FAILURE);                                                 \\\n        }                                                                       \\\n    } while (0)\n\n// Threads per block for the update kernel\nconstexpr int BLOCK_SIZE = 256;\n\n// Device-side mesh in structure-of-arrays form.\n//\n// The host representation (array of ElementStatic) is convenient to build but\n// gives strided, cache-hostile access on a GPU: every thread would touch a\n// 144-byte record. On the device the same information is stored as separate\n// arrays, with the per-connection data transposed so that connection j of all\n// elements is contiguous (conn[j * n + i]). Consecutive threads therefore read\n// consecutive addresses for every load, which is what the memory system wants.\nstruct DeviceMesh {\n    uint32_t n_elems = 0;\n    uint32_t max_conn = 0;              // Largest num_connections in the mesh\n\n    Material* materials = nullptr;      // [n_materials]\n    uint32_t* material_idx = nullptr;   // [n_elems]\n    uint8_t* num_conn = nullptr;        // [n_elems]\n    uint32_t* conn_idx = nullptr;       // [max_conn * n_elems], transposed\n    val_t* conn_flux = nullptr;         // [max_conn * n_elems], transposed\n\n    val_t* energy[2] = {nullptr, nullptr};  // Ping-pong energy buffers\n    val_t* flux = nullptr;                  // Accumulated |flux| per element\n};\n\n// Compute energy flux between two elements\n__host__ __device__ inline val_t computeFlux(const Material& mat, val_t this_energy,\n                                             val_t connection_flux, val_t other_energy) {\n    return (other_energy - this_energy) *\n           mat.transfer_coeff * connection_flux * 0.25;\n}\n\n// One simulation step: one thread per element, grid-stride so that any mesh\n// size maps onto any launch configuration.\n//\n// The accumulated flux is updated in place: a thread only ever touches its own\n// element for that quantity, so no second buffer (and no extra traffic) is\n// needed. Energy is double-buffered because neighbours read it.\n__global__ __launch_bounds__(BLOCK_SIZE) void updateElementsKernel(\n    const uint32_t n_elems,\n    const val_t* __restrict__ energy_in,\n    val_t* __restrict__ energy_out,\n    val_t* __restrict__ flux_acc,\n    const uint32_t* __restrict__ conn_idx,\n    const val_t* __restrict__ conn_flux,\n    const uint8_t* __restrict__ num_conn,\n    const uint32_t* __restrict__ material_idx,\n    const Material* __restrict__ materials) {\n    const uint32_t stride = blockDim.x * gridDim.x;\n\n    for (uint32_t i = blockIdx.x * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x; i < n_elems; i += stride) {\n        const Material mat = materials[material_idx[i]];\n        const val_t energy = energy_in[i];\n\n        // Start with external flow\n        val_t total_flux = mat.external_flow;\n\n        // Gather the connectivity of this element first. The loop is fully\n        // unrolled and predicated so the temporaries stay in registers; this\n        // puts all connectivity loads in flight at once instead of serializing\n        // them behind the dependent neighbour gather below.\n        const uint32_t nc = num_conn[i];\n        uint32_t nbr[MAX_CONNECTIONS];\n        val_t cflux[MAX_CONNECTIONS];\n#pragma unroll\n        for (int j = 0; j < MAX_CONNECTIONS; ++j) {\n            if (static_cast<uint32_t>(j) < nc) {\n                const uint32_t base = j * n_elems + i;\n                nbr[j] = conn_idx[base];\n                cflux[j] = conn_flux[base];\n            }\n        }\n\n        // Neighbour energies: independent loads, again all issued together.\n        val_t nrg[MAX_CONNECTIONS];\n#pragma unroll\n        for (int j = 0; j < MAX_CONNECTIONS; ++j) {\n            if (static_cast<uint32_t>(j) < nc) {\n                nrg[j] = __ldg(&energy_in[nbr[j]]);\n            }\n        }\n\n        // Add flux from all connected elements (same order as the reference\n        // implementation, so the floating-point result is bit-identical)\n#pragma unroll\n        for (int j = 0; j < MAX_CONNECTIONS; ++j) {\n            if (static_cast<uint32_t>(j) < nc) {\n                total_flux += computeFlux(mat, energy, cflux[j], nrg[j]);\n            }\n        }\n\n        // Update element state\n        energy_out[i] = energy + total_flux;\n        flux_acc[i] += fabs(total_flux);\n    }\n}\n\n// Build a 2D square grid as an unstructured mesh\n// This represents computation on arbitrarily-shaped geometries\nvoid buildSquare2D(World& world, const int n_elems_root) {\n    const int n_elems = n_elems_root * n_elems_root;\n\n    // Initialize materials\n    world.materials.emplace_back(Material{0.8, 0.0});    // Default material\n    world.materials.emplace_back(Material{0.8, 0.5});    // Inflow material\n    world.materials.emplace_back(Material{0.8, -0.5});   // Outflow material\n\n    // Allocate elements\n    world.elements_static.resize(n_elems);\n    world.elements_dynamic.resize(n_elems);\n    world.elements_dynamic_swap.resize(n_elems);\n\n    // Initialize all elements with default material and zero energy\n    for (int i = 0; i < n_elems; ++i) {\n        world.elements_static[i].material_idx = DEFAULT_MAT_ID;\n        world.elements_static[i].num_connections = 0;\n        world.elements_dynamic[i].current_energy = 0.0;\n        world.elements_dynamic[i].total_flux = 0.0;\n    }\n\n    // Build connectivity: each element connects to its neighbors in 2D grid\n    for (int x = 0; x < n_elems_root; ++x) {\n        for (int y = 0; y < n_elems_root; ++y) {\n            const int idx = x * n_elems_root + y;\n            ElementStatic& elem = world.elements_static[idx];\n\n            // Connect to neighbors (up, down, left, right)\n            const int offsets[4][2] = {{1, 0}, {-1, 0}, {0, 1}, {0, -1}};\n\n            for (int n = 0; n < 4; ++n) {\n                const int nx = x + offsets[n][0];\n                const int ny = y + offsets[n][1];\n\n                // Check if neighbor is within bounds\n                if (nx >= 0 && nx < n_elems_root && ny >= 0 && ny < n_elems_root) {\n                    const int neighbor_idx = nx * n_elems_root + ny;\n                    elem.connected_idx[elem.num_connections] = neighbor_idx;\n                    elem.connected_flux[elem.num_connections] = 1.0;\n                    elem.num_connections++;\n                }\n            }\n        }\n    }\n\n    // Set corner elements as inflow/outflow to create interesting dynamics\n    const int last = n_elems_root - 1;\n    world.elements_static[0 * n_elems_root + 0].material_idx = INFLOW_MAT_ID;\n    world.elements_static[0 * n_elems_root + last].material_idx = OUTFLOW_MAT_ID;\n    world.elements_static[last * n_elems_root + 0].material_idx = OUTFLOW_MAT_ID;\n    world.elements_static[last * n_elems_root + last].material_idx = INFLOW_MAT_ID;\n}\n\n// Transfer the static mesh (connectivity, materials) to the device.\n// This is part of problem setup, like building the mesh itself, and is\n// therefore reported separately from the simulation time.\nvoid uploadMesh(const World& world, DeviceMesh& mesh) {\n    const size_t n_elems = world.elements_static.size();\n    if (n_elems > std::numeric_limits<uint32_t>::max()) {\n        fprintf(stderr, \"Mesh too large for 32-bit element indices\\n\");\n        exit(EXIT_FAILURE);\n    }\n    mesh.n_elems = static_cast<uint32_t>(n_elems);\n\n    // Repack connectivity into transposed structure-of-arrays form\n    size_t max_conn = 1;\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < n_elems; ++i) {\n        max_conn = std::max<size_t>(max_conn, world.elements_static[i].num_connections);\n    }\n    mesh.max_conn = static_cast<uint32_t>(max_conn);\n\n    std::vector<uint32_t> h_material_idx(n_elems);\n    std::vector<uint8_t> h_num_conn(n_elems);\n    std::vector<uint32_t> h_conn_idx(max_conn * n_elems, 0);\n    std::vector<val_t> h_conn_flux(max_conn * n_elems, 0.0);\n\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < n_elems; ++i) {\n        const ElementStatic& elem = world.elements_static[i];\n        h_material_idx[i] = static_cast<uint32_t>(elem.material_idx);\n        h_num_conn[i] = static_cast<uint8_t>(elem.num_connections);\n        for (idx_t j = 0; j < elem.num_connections; ++j) {\n            h_conn_idx[j * n_elems + i] = static_cast<uint32_t>(elem.connected_idx[j]);\n            h_conn_flux[j * n_elems + i] = elem.connected_flux[j];\n        }\n    }\n\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&mesh.materials, world.materials.size() * sizeof(Material)));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&mesh.material_idx, n_elems * sizeof(uint32_t)));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&mesh.num_conn, n_elems * sizeof(uint8_t)));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&mesh.conn_idx, h_conn_idx.size() * sizeof(uint32_t)));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&mesh.conn_flux, h_conn_flux.size() * sizeof(val_t)));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&mesh.energy[0], n_elems * sizeof(val_t)));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&mesh.energy[1], n_elems * sizeof(val_t)));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&mesh.flux, n_elems * sizeof(val_t)));\n\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(mesh.materials, world.materials.data(),\n                          world.materials.size() * sizeof(Material), cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(mesh.material_idx, h_material_idx.data(),\n                          n_elems * sizeof(uint32_t), cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(mesh.num_conn, h_num_conn.data(),\n                          n_elems * sizeof(uint8_t), cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(mesh.conn_idx, h_conn_idx.data(),\n                          h_conn_idx.size() * sizeof(uint32_t), cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(mesh.conn_flux, h_conn_flux.data(),\n                          h_conn_flux.size() * sizeof(val_t), cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));\n\n    // Warm up: force the kernel module to be loaded now, so that one-time\n    // runtime initialization is not attributed to the simulation. The launch\n    // itself is a no-op (zero elements).\n    updateElementsKernel<<<1, BLOCK_SIZE>>>(0, mesh.energy[0], mesh.energy[1], mesh.flux,\n                                            mesh.conn_idx, mesh.conn_flux, mesh.num_conn,\n                                            mesh.material_idx, mesh.materials);\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetLastError());\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaDeviceSynchronize());\n}\n\nvoid freeMesh(DeviceMesh& mesh) {\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(mesh.materials));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(mesh.material_idx));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(mesh.num_conn));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(mesh.conn_idx));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(mesh.conn_flux));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(mesh.energy[0]));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(mesh.energy[1]));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(mesh.flux));\n    mesh = DeviceMesh{};\n}\n\n// Run simulation for n_iters iterations on the GPU\nvoid runSimulation(World& world, DeviceMesh& mesh, const int n_iters) {\n    const uint32_t n_elems = mesh.n_elems;\n\n    // Upload the initial dynamic state (energy / accumulated flux)\n    std::vector<val_t> h_energy(n_elems);\n    std::vector<val_t> h_flux(n_elems);\n    for (uint32_t i = 0; i < n_elems; ++i) {\n        h_energy[i] = world.elements_dynamic[i].current_energy;\n        h_flux[i] = world.elements_dynamic[i].total_flux;\n    }\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(mesh.energy[0], h_energy.data(), n_elems * sizeof(val_t),\n                          cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(mesh.flux, h_flux.data(), n_elems * sizeof(val_t),\n                          cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));\n\n    const int blocks = static_cast<int>((n_elems + BLOCK_SIZE - 1) / BLOCK_SIZE);\n\n    int cur = 0;\n    for (int iter = 0; iter < n_iters; ++iter) {\n        updateElementsKernel<<<blocks, BLOCK_SIZE>>>(\n            n_elems, mesh.energy[cur], mesh.energy[cur ^ 1], mesh.flux,\n            mesh.conn_idx, mesh.conn_flux, mesh.num_conn,\n            mesh.material_idx, mesh.materials);\n        cur ^= 1;\n    }\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetLastError());\n\n    // Bring auto& elem : world.elements_dynamic) {\n        energy_sum += elem.current_energy;\n        flux_sum += elem.total_flux;\n        energy_max = std::max(elem.current_energy, energy_max);\n        energy_min = std::min(elem.current_energy, energy_min);\n    }\n\n    printf(\"Validation results:\\n\");\n    printf(\"  Energy sum: %.12f\\n\", energy_sum);\n    printf(\"  Flux sum: %.2f\\n\", Consecutive threads therefore read\n79\t// consecutive addresses for every load, which is what the memory system wants.\n80\tstruct DeviceMesh {\n81\t    uint32_t n_elems = 0;\n82\t    uint32_t max_conn = 0;              // Largest num_connections in the mesh\n83\t\n84\t    Material* materials = nullptr;      // [n_materials]\n85\t    uint32_t* material_idx = nullptr;   // [n_elems]\n86\t    uint8_t* num_conn = nullptr;        // [n_elems]\n87\t    uint32_t* conn_idx = nullptr;       // [max_conn * n_elems], transposed\n88\t    val_t* conn_flux = nullptr;         // [max_conn * n_elems], transposed\n89\t\n90\t    val_t* energy[2] = {nullptr, nullptr};  // Ping-pong energy buffers\n91\t    val_t* flux = nullptr;                  // Accumulated |flux| per element\n92\t};\n93\t\n94\t// Compute energy flux between two elements\n95\t__host__ __device__ inline val_t computeFlux(const Material& mat, val_t this_energy,\n96\t                                             val_t connection_flux, val_t other_energy) {\n97\t    return (other_energy - this_energy) *\n98\t           mat.transfer_coeff * connection_flux * 0.25;\n99\t}\n100\t\n101\t// One simulation step: one thread per element, grid-stride so that any mesh\n102\t// size maps onto any launch configuration.\n103\t//\n104\t// The accumulated flux is updated in place: a thread only ever touches its own\n105\t// element for that quantity, so no second buffer (and no extra traffic) is\n106\t// needed. Consecutive threads therefore read\n// consecutive addresses for every load, which is what the memory system wants.\nstruct DeviceMesh {\n    uint32_t n_elems = 0;\n    uint32_t max_conn = 0;              // Largest num_connections in the mesh\n\n    Material* materials = nullptr;      // [n_materials]\n    uint32_t* material_idx = nullptr;   // [n_elems]\n    uint8_t* num_conn = nullptr;        // [n_elems]\n    uint32_t* conn_idx = nullptr;       // [max_conn * n_elems], transposed\n    val_t* conn_flux = nullptr;         // [max_conn * n_elems], transposed\n\n    val_t* energy[2] = {nullptr, nullptr};  // Ping-pong energy buffers\n    val_t* flux = nullptr;                  // Accumulated |flux| per element\n};\n\n// Compute energy flux between two elements\n__host__ __device__ inline val_t computeFlux(const Material& mat, val_t this_energy,\n                                             val_t connection_flux, val_t other_energy) {\n    return (other_energy - this_energy) *\n           mat.transfer_coeff * connection_flux * 0.25;\n}\n\n// One simulation step: one thread per element, grid-stride so that any mesh\n// size maps onto any launch configuration.\n//\n// The accumulated flux is updated in place: a thread only ever touches its own\n// element for that quantity, so no second buffer (and no extra traffic) is\n// needed.    // Default material\n    world.materials.emplace_back(Material{0.8, 0.5});    // Inflow material\n    world.materials.emplace_back(Material{0.8, -0.5});   // Outflow material\n\n    // Allocate elements\n    world.elements_static.resize(n_elems);\n    world.elements_dynamic.resize(n_elems);\n    world.elements_dynamic_swap.resize(n_elems);\n\n    // Initialize all elements with default material and zero energy\n    for (int i = 0; i < n_elems; ++i) {\n        world.elements_static[i].material_idx = DEFAULT_MAT_ID;\n        world.elements_static[i].num_connections = 0;\n        world.elements_dynamic[i].current_energy = 0.0;\n        world.elements_dynamic[i].total_flux = 0.0;\n    }\n\n    // Build connectivity: each element connects to its neighbors in 2D grid\n    for (int x = 0; n < 4; ++n) {\n                const int nx = x + offsets[n][0];\n                const int ny = y + offsets[n][1];\n\n                // Check if neighbor is within bounds\n                if (nx >= 0 && nx < n_elems_root && ny >= 0 && ny < n_elems_root) {\n                    const int neighbor_idx = nx * n_elems_root + ny;\n                    elem.connected_idx[elem.num_connections] = neighbor_idx;\n                    elem.connected_flux[elem.num_connections] = 1.0;\n                    elem.num_connections++;\n                }\n            }\n        }\n    }\n\n    // Set corner elements as inflow/outflow to create interesting dynamics\n    const int last = n_elems_root - 1;\n    world.elements_static[0 * n_elems_root + 0].material_idx = INFLOW_MAT_ID;\n    world.elements_static[0 * n_elems_root + last].material_idx = OUTFLOW_MAT_ID;\n    world.elements_static[last * n_elems_root + 0].material_idx = OUTFLOW_MAT_ID;\n    world.elements_static[last * n_elems_root + last].material_idx = INFLOW_MAT_ID;\n}\n\n// Transfer the static mesh (connectivity, materials) to the device.\n// This is part of problem setup, like building the mesh itself, and is\n// therefore reported separately from the simulation time.\nvoid uploadMesh(const World& world, DeviceMesh& mesh) {\n    const size_t n_elems = world.elements_static.size();\n    if (n_elems > std::numeric_limits<uint32_t>::max()) {\n        fprintf(stderr, \"Mesh too large for 32-bit element indices\\n\");\n        exit(EXIT_FAILURE);\n    }\n    mesh.n_elems = static_cast<uint32_t>(n_elems);\n\n    // Repack connectivity into transposed structure-of-arrays form\n    size_t max_conn = 1;\n    for (size_t i = 0;
